  Rule: a-specific-attribute takes the-NN_ of the-generic-attribute it refines;
        a-specific WITHOUT an-attribute inherits it, it is NOT an-issue.

SUMMARY
  INH-NONUM         0  matches a-numbered generic-attribute, has no NN_ (--fix adds it, if NN free)
  INH-WRONGNUM      0  its NN_ differs from the-generic-attribute (own numbering? by hand)
  INH-OWNNUM        0  an-own numbered attribute NOT right after the-last number of the-inherited-attr-chain
  INH-OWNNONUM   1512  an-own attribute without number: it gets the-next NN after the-chain (--fix writes it)
  INH-LAST          0  a-generic without (or with an-old) name Mmn'NN_last: NN the-last number its specifics continue from (--fix writes it)
  INH-ORDER         0  the-attributes of a-specific are not in the-NN order of its generic
  INH-EMPTY       140  an-inherited attribute that says nothing new (no info, no link): delete?
  INH-GENDUP        0  a-numbered generic gives the-same NN_ to two attributes: renumber
  INH-LOCAL       116  an-attribute in 3+ specifics, not in the-generic: lift it up?
  INH-REFBROKEN    11  a-text-reference name!@File#id whose file or id does NOT exist
  INH-GNTRTEXT     86  a-generic "X:generic" in generic-tree:: that does NOT resolve to one concept
  INH-TITLENAME     0  a-numbered attribute without its title X'NN_attr as name (--fix-name writes it)
  INH-TITLEHAND     8  a-numbered attribute whose title-name needs a-hand
